    ' * the sandboxed admin and unsandboxed sites-home surfaces separate. The sites home window cannot use that preload (it needs `tovuRunner` too), so rather than\n' +

    'function subscribe<Payload>({ channel, listener }: { channel: string; listener: (payload: Payload) => void }, _options = {}): () => void {\n' +
    '  const handler = (_event: unknown, payload: Payload): void => listener(payload);\n' +
    '  ipcRenderer.on(channel, handler);\n' +
    '  return () => {\n' +
    '    ipcRenderer.removeListener(channel, handler);\n' +
    '  };\n' +
    '}\n' +

    '// Separate from the inventory bridge so S4 can adopt the typed API without changing site tools.\n' +

    ' *   That IS the site switcher: no custom panel was needed, every open site is just another window.\n' +
    " *   The sites home UI's own switcher is its tab strip instead (`renderer/app/TabStrip.tsx`) — one window, N tabs.\n" +
